A=\frac{2\sqrt{KK_{1d}}}{\sqrt{KK_{2d}}+K_{1d}}
.
Recall from
Theorem

5.1
that
\lambda_{i}=\left(\gamma\chi^{2}(\mu_{i}\|\pi)Z\right)^{\frac{1}{r+1}}\wedge 1
for
i=1,\ldots,n
where
Z
is the constant that depends on
\beta,\zeta,K_{1d},K_{2d},Q,\mathcal{J},\mathcal{I}
.
From the condition on the number of samples
M
and
N
in (
25
), we obtain that if
\tilde{\lambda}=\lambda_{j}=\left(\gamma\chi^{2}(\mu_{j}\|\pi)Z\right)^{\frac{1}{r+1}}
for some
j\in\{1,\ldots,n\}
,

\displaystyle\sqrt{M}

\displaystyle\gtrsim\left(\frac{1}{\gamma}\right)\left(\frac{1}{\min\limits_{i=1,\ldots,n}\operatorname{KL}(\mu_{i}\|\pi)Z\wedge 1}\right)^{\frac{1}{r+1}}\exp\left(\frac{4n\gamma^{\frac{r}{r+1}}R}{\left(\min\limits_{i=1,\ldots,n}\operatorname{KL}(\mu_{i}\|\pi)Z\right)^{\frac{1}{r+1}}\wedge 1}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{\gamma}\left(\frac{1}{\min\limits_{i=1,\ldots,n}\operatorname{KL}(\mu_{i}\|\pi)Z}\right)^{\frac{1}{r+1}}\exp\left(\frac{4n\gamma^{\frac{r}{r+1}}R}{\left(\min\limits_{i=1,\ldots,n}\operatorname{KL}(\mu_{i}\|\pi)Z\right)^{\frac{1}{r+1}}}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{\gamma}\left(\frac{1}{\operatorname{KL}(\mu_{j}\|\pi)Z}\right)^{\frac{1}{r+1}}\exp\left(\frac{4n\gamma^{\frac{r}{r+1}}R}{\left(\operatorname{KL}(\mu_{j}\|\pi)Z\right)^{\frac{1}{r+1}}}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{\gamma}\left(\frac{1}{\chi^{2}(\mu_{j}\|\pi)Z}\right)^{\frac{1}{r+1}}\exp\left(\frac{4n\gamma^{\frac{r}{r+1}}R}{\left(\chi^{2}(\mu_{j}\|\pi)Z\right)^{\frac{1}{r+1}}}\right)

\displaystyle=A\gamma^{-\frac{r}{r+1}}\frac{1}{\tilde{\lambda}}\exp\left(\frac{4n\gamma R}{\tilde{\lambda}}\right)\geq\gamma^{-\frac{r}{2r+2}}\frac{1}{\tilde{\lambda}}\exp\left(\frac{4n\gamma R}{\tilde{\lambda}}\right).

On the other hand, if
\tilde{\lambda}=1
, since
\gamma\leq 1
,

\displaystyle\sqrt{M}

\displaystyle\gtrsim\left(\frac{1}{\gamma}\right)\left(\frac{1}{\min\limits_{i=1,\ldots,n}\operatorname{KL}(\mu_{i}\|\pi)Z\wedge 1}\right)^{\frac{1}{r+1}}\exp\left(\frac{4n\gamma^{\frac{r}{r+1}}R}{\left(\min\limits_{i=1,\ldots,n}\operatorname{KL}(\mu_{i}\|\pi)Z\right)^{\frac{1}{r+1}}\wedge 1}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{\gamma}\exp\left(4n\gamma^{\frac{r}{r+1}}R\right)\geq\gamma^{-\frac{r}{2r+2}}\exp\left(4n\gamma R\right)=\gamma^{-\frac{r}{2r+2}}\frac{1}{\tilde{\lambda}}\exp\left(\frac{4n\gamma R}{\tilde{\lambda}}\right).

Similarly for
N
, we have

\displaystyle\sqrt{N}

\displaystyle\gtrsim\gamma^{-\frac{r}{2r+2}}\exp\left(\frac{4n\gamma R}{\tilde{\lambda}}\right)\quad\text{and}\quad N^{-\frac{1}{d\vee 4}}\lesssim\gamma^{\frac{r}{2r+2}}.

Plugging them back to (
65
), we obtain

\displaystyle\E\left[W_{2}\left(\hat{\mu}_{n},\pi\right)\right]\leq\sqrt{2C_{T}}\exp\left(-\frac{n\gamma}{C_{P}}\right)\sqrt{\operatorname{KL}\left(\mu_{0}\|\pi\right)}+\mathcal{O}\left(\gamma^{\frac{r}{2r+2}}\right),

which completes the proof.

10.13
Proof of
Proposition

10.1

Since the proof below works for any regularization coefficient
\lambda
, we use a fixed
\lambda
for the majority of the analysis and resort back to adaptive
\lambda_{n}
at the end of the proof.
For empirical distributions
\hat{\mu}_{n}=\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}y_{n}^{(i)}
and
\bar{\mu}_{n}=\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\overline{y}_{n}^{(i)}
defined in (
24
) and (
64
), note that

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \E W_{2}^{2}(\hat{\mu}_{n},\bar{\mu}_{n})\leq\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\E\left[\left\|y_{n}^{(i)}-\overline{y}_{n}^{(i)}\right\|^{2}\right]:=c_{n}^{2}.\end{aligned}

Consider

\displaystyle c_{n+1}

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\E\left[\left\|y_{n+1}^{(i)}-\overline{y}_{n+1}^{(i)}\right\|^{2}\right]}

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\E\left[\left\|y_{n}^{(i)}-\overline{y}_{n}^{(i)}-\gamma(1+\lambda)\left(\nabla h_{\hat{\mu}_{n},\hat{\pi}}^{\ast}(y_{n}^{(i)})-\nabla h_{{\mu}_{n},{\pi}}^{\ast}(\overline{y}_{n}^{(i)})\right)\right\|^{2}\right]}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\E\left\|y_{n}^{(i)}-\overline{y}_{n}^{(i)}\right\|^{2}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\sqrt{\frac{1}{N}\E\sum_{i=1}^{N}\left\|\gamma(1+\lambda)\left(\nabla h_{\hat{\mu}_{n},\hat{\pi}}^{\ast}(y_{n}^{(i)})-\nabla h_{{\mu}_{n},{\pi}}^{\ast}(\overline{y}_{n}^{(i)})\right)\right\|^{2}\ }

\displaystyle=c_{n}+\frac{\gamma(1+\lambda)}{\sqrt{N}}\sqrt{\sum_{i=1}^{N}\E\left\|\nabla h_{\hat{\mu}_{n},\hat{\pi}}^{\ast}(y_{n}^{(i)})-\nabla h_{{\mu}_{n},{\pi}}^{\ast}(\overline{y}_{n}^{(i)})\right\|^{2}},

where we used Minkowski’s inequality,

\displaystyle\sqrt{\sum_{i=1}^{N}\|a_{i}+b_{i}\|^{2}}\leq\sqrt{\sum_{i=1}^{N}\|a_{i}\|^{2}}+\sqrt{\sum_{i=1}^{N}\|b_{i}\|^{2}}

in the above inequalities.
Again, by Minkowski’s inequality, we have

\displaystyle c_{n+1}\leq c_{n}+\gamma(1+\lambda)\Bigg(\underbrace{\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\sqrt{\sum_{i=1}^{N}\E\left[\left\|\nabla h_{\hat{\mu}_{n},\hat{\pi}}^{\ast}(y_{n}^{(i)})-\nabla h_{\hat{\mu}_{n},\hat{\pi}}^{\ast}(\overline{y}_{n}^{(i)})\right\|^{2}\right]}}_{(i)}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\sqrt{\sum_{i=1}^{N}\E\left[\left\|\nabla h_{\hat{\mu}_{n},\hat{\pi}}^{\ast}(\overline{y}_{n}^{(i)})-\nabla h_{{\mu}_{n},{\pi}}^{\ast}(\overline{y}_{n}^{(i)})\right\|^{2}\right]}}_{(ii)}\Bigg).

10.13.1
Controlling
(i)
:

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{N}\E\left\|\nabla h_{\hat{\mu}_{n},\hat{\pi}}^{\ast}(y_{n}^{(i)})-\nabla h_{\hat{\mu}_{n},\hat{\pi}}^{\ast}(\overline{y}_{n}^{(i)})\right\|^{2}=\sum_{i=1}^{N}\E\left[\sum_{j=1}^{d}{\left\langle\partial_{j}k(y_{n}^{(i)},\cdot)-\partial_{j}k(\overline{y}_{n}^{(i)},\cdot),h_{\hat{\mu}_{n},\hat{\pi}}^{\ast}\right\rangle}_{\!\mathcal{H}}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i=1}^{N}\E\left[\sum_{j=1}^{d}\left\|\partial_{j}k(y_{n}^{(i)},\cdot)-\partial_{j}k(\overline{y}_{n}^{(i)},\cdot)\right\|_{\mathcal{H}}^{2}\right]\left\|h_{\hat{\mu}_{n},\hat{\pi}}^{\ast}\right\|_{\mathcal{H}}^{2}\leq\frac{4KK_{2d}}{\lambda^{2}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\E\left\|y_{n}^{(i)}-\overline{y}_{n}^{(i)}\right\|^{2},

where the second inequality uses Cauchy-Schwarz inequality and the third follows from using
Lemma

B.3
and
Lemma

B.4
.
So we have

\displaystyle(i)=\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\sqrt{\sum_{i=1}^{N}\E\left\|\nabla h_{\hat{\mu}_{n},\hat{\pi}}^{\ast}(y_{n}^{(i)})-\nabla h_{\hat{\mu}_{n},\hat{\pi}}^{\ast}(\overline{y}_{n}^{(i)})\right\|^{2}}\leq\frac{2\sqrt{KK_{2d}}}{\lambda}c_{n}.

10.13.2
Controlling
(ii)
:

First, we introduce some auxiliary witness functions,

\displaystyle h_{\bar{\mu}_{n},\hat{\pi}}^{\ast}=2\left(\Sigma_{\hat{\pi}}+\lambda\mathrm{I}\right)^{-1}\left(m_{\bar{\mu}_{n}}-m_{\hat{\pi}}\right),\quad\overline{\overline{h}}_{n}^{\ast}=2\left(\Sigma_{\pi}+\lambda\mathrm{I}\right)^{-1}\left(m_{\bar{\mu}_{n}}-m_{\hat{\pi}}\right),

and for completeness, we recall the witness function we are interested in:

\displaystyle h_{{\mu}_{n},{\pi}}^{\ast}=2\left(\Sigma_{\pi}+\lambda\mathrm{I}\right)^{-1}\left(m_{\mu_{n}}-m_{\pi}\right),\quad h_{\hat{\mu}_{n},\hat{\pi}}^{\ast}=2\left(\Sigma_{\hat{\pi}}+\lambda\mathrm{I}\right)^{-1}\left(m_{\hat{\mu}_{n}}-m_{\hat{\pi}}\right).

We know that

\displaystyle(ii)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\sqrt{\sum_{i=1}^{N}\E\left[\left\|\nabla h_{\hat{\mu}_{n},\hat{\pi}}^{\ast}(\overline{y}_{n}^{(i)})-\nabla h_{{\mu}_{n},{\pi}}^{\ast}(\overline{y}_{n}^{(i)})\right\|^{2}\right]}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\sqrt{\sum_{i=1}^{N}\E\left[\left\|\nabla h_{\hat{\mu}_{n},\hat{\pi}}^{\ast}(\overline{y}_{n}^{(i)})-\nabla h_{\bar{\mu}_{n},\hat{\pi}}^{\ast}(\overline{y}_{n}^{(i)})\right\|^{2}\right]}

\displaystyle\quad\qquad+\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\sqrt{\sum_{i=1}^{N}\E\left[\left\|\nabla h_{\bar{\mu}_{n},\hat{\pi}}^{\ast}(\overline{y}_{n}^{(i)})-\nabla\overline{\overline{h}}_{n}^{\ast}(\overline{y}_{n}^{(i)})\right\|^{2}\right]}

\displaystyle\quad\qquad\quad\qquad+\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\sqrt{\sum_{i=1}^{N}\E\left[\left\|\nabla\overline{\overline{h}}_{n}^{\ast}(\overline{y}_{n}^{(i)})-\nabla h_{{\mu}_{n},{\pi}}^{\ast}(\overline{y}_{n}^{(i)})\right\|^{2}\right]}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\Bigg(\sqrt{\sum_{i=1}^{N}K_{1d}\E\left\|h_{\hat{\mu}_{n},\hat{\pi}}^{\ast}-h_{\bar{\mu}_{n},\hat{\pi}}^{\ast}\right\|_{\mathcal{H}}^{2}}+\sqrt{\sum_{i=1}^{N}K_{1d}\E\left\|h_{\bar{\mu}_{n},\hat{\pi}}^{\ast}-\overline{\overline{h}}_{n}^{\ast}\right\|_{\mathcal{H}}^{2}}

\displaystyle\quad\qquad+\sqrt{\sum_{i=1}^{N}K_{1d}\E\left\|\overline{\overline{h}}_{n}^{\ast}-h_{{\mu}_{n},{\pi}}^{\ast}\right\|_{\mathcal{H}}^{2}}\Bigg)

\displaystyle=\sqrt{K_{1d}}\left(\sqrt{\E\left\|h_{\hat{\mu}_{n},\hat{\pi}}^{\ast}-h_{\bar{\mu}_{n},\hat{\pi}}^{\ast}\right\|_{\mathcal{H}}^{2}}+\sqrt{\E\left\|h_{\bar{\mu}_{n},\hat{\pi}}^{\ast}-\overline{\overline{h}}_{n}^{\ast}\right\|_{\mathcal{H}}^{2}}+\sqrt{\E\left\|\overline{\overline{h}}_{n}^{\ast}-h_{{\mu}_{n},{\pi}}^{\ast}\right\|_{\mathcal{H}}^{2}}\right),

where the first inequality follows from Minkowski’s inequality, and the second inequality uses the fact that for
h_{0},h_{1}\in\mathcal{H}
,

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \left\|\nabla h_{1}-\nabla h_{0}\right\|_{\mathcal{H}^{d}}^{2}\leq\left\|\nabla_{1}k(x,\cdot)\right\|_{\mathcal{H}^{d}}^{2}\left\|h_{1}-h_{0}\right\|_{\mathcal{H}}^{2}\leq K_{1d}\left\|h_{1}-h_{0}\right\|_{\mathcal{H}}^{2}.\\
\end{aligned}

Next, we will bound
\sqrt{\E\|h_{\hat{\mu}_{n},\hat{\pi}}^{\ast}-h_{\bar{\mu}_{n},\hat{\pi}}^{\ast}\|_{\mathcal{H}}^{2}},\sqrt{\E\|h_{\bar{\mu}_{n},\hat{\pi}}^{\ast}-\overline{\overline{h}}_{n}^{\ast}\|_{\mathcal{H}}^{2}}
, and
\sqrt{\E\|\overline{\overline{h}}_{n}^{\ast}-h_{{\mu}_{n},{\pi}}^{\ast}\|_{\mathcal{H}}^{2}}
separately.

First, by noticing that
h_{\hat{\mu}_{n},\hat{\pi}}^{\ast}=2\left(\Sigma_{\hat{\pi}}+\lambda\mathrm{I}\right)^{-1}\left(m_{\hat{\mu}_{n}}-m_{\hat{\pi}}\right)
is the witness function associated with
\operatorname{DrMMD}(\hat{\mu}_{n}||\hat{\pi})
, and
h_{\bar{\mu}_{n},\hat{\pi}}^{\ast}=2\left(\Sigma_{\hat{\pi}}+\lambda\mathrm{I}\right)^{-1}\left(m_{\bar{\mu}_{n}}-m_{\hat{\pi}}\right)
is the witness function associated with
\operatorname{DrMMD}(\bar{\mu}_{n}||\hat{\pi})
, by using
Lemma

B.4
, we have

\displaystyle\sqrt{\E\left\|h_{\hat{\mu}_{n},\hat{\pi}}^{\ast}-h_{\bar{\mu}_{n},\hat{\pi}}^{\ast}\right\|_{\mathcal{H}}^{2}}\leq\sqrt{\E\frac{4K_{1d}}{\lambda^{2}}W_{2}^{2}\left(\bar{\mu}_{n},\hat{\mu}_{n}\right)}\leq\frac{2\sqrt{K_{1d}}}{\lambda}c_{n}.

(66)

Second,

\displaystyle\sqrt{\E\left\|h_{\bar{\mu}_{n},\hat{\pi}}^{\ast}-\overline{\overline{h}}_{n}^{\ast}\right\|_{\mathcal{H}}^{2}}=\sqrt{\E\left\|2\left(\Sigma_{\hat{\pi}}+\lambda\mathrm{I}\right)^{-1}\left(m_{\bar{\mu}_{n}}-m_{\hat{\pi}}\right)-2\left(\Sigma_{\pi}+\lambda\mathrm{I}\right)^{-1}\left(m_{\bar{\mu}_{n}}-m_{\hat{\pi}}\right)\right\|_{\mathcal{H}}^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq 2\sqrt{\E\left\|\left(\Sigma_{\hat{\pi}}+\lambda\mathrm{I}\right)^{-1}-\left(\Sigma_{\pi}+\lambda\mathrm{I}\right)^{-1}\right\|_{\operatorname{HS}}^{2}\left\|m_{\bar{\mu}_{n}}-m_{\hat{\pi}}\right\|_{\mathcal{H}}^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq 4\sqrt{K}\sqrt{\E\left\|\left(\Sigma_{\hat{\pi}}+\lambda\mathrm{I}\right)^{-1}-\left(\Sigma_{\pi}+\lambda\mathrm{I}\right)^{-1}\right\|^{2}_{\operatorname{HS}}}

\displaystyle=4\sqrt{K}\sqrt{\E\left\|\left(\Sigma_{\hat{\pi}}+\lambda\mathrm{I}\right)^{-1}\Big((\Sigma_{\hat{\pi}}+\lambda\mathrm{I})-(\Sigma_{\pi}+\lambda\mathrm{I})\Big)\left(\Sigma_{\pi}+\lambda\mathrm{I}\right)^{-1}\right\|^{2}_{\operatorname{HS}}}

\displaystyle\leq 4\sqrt{K}\frac{1}{\lambda^{2}}\sqrt{\E\left\|\Sigma_{\hat{\pi}}-\Sigma_{\pi}\right\|^{2}_{\operatorname{HS}}}\leq 4\sqrt{K}\frac{1}{\lambda^{2}}\sqrt{\frac{K^{2}}{M}},

(67)

where the last inequality follows from using
Lemma

B.8
and the fact that
\|k(x,\cdot)\otimes k(x,\cdot)\|_{\operatorname{HS}}\leq K
.

Third,

\displaystyle\sqrt{\E\left\|\overline{\overline{h}}_{n}^{\ast}-h_{{\mu}_{n},{\pi}}^{\ast}\right\|_{\mathcal{H}}^{2}}=\sqrt{\E\left\|2\left(\Sigma_{\pi}+\lambda\mathrm{I}\right)^{-1}\left(m_{\bar{\mu}_{n}}-m_{\hat{\pi}}\right)-2\left(\Sigma_{\pi}+\lambda\mathrm{I}\right)^{-1}\left(m_{\mu_{n}}-m_{\pi}\right)\right\|_{\mathcal{H}}^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2}{\lambda}\sqrt{\E\left\|\left(m_{\bar{\mu}_{n}}-m_{\hat{\pi}}\right)-\left(m_{\mu_{n}}-m_{\pi}\right)\right\|_{\mathcal{H}}^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2}{\lambda}\left(\sqrt{\E\left\|m_{\bar{\mu}_{n}}-m_{\mu_{n}}\right\|_{\mathcal{H}}^{2}}+\sqrt{\E\left\|m_{\hat{\pi}}-m_{\pi}\right\|_{\mathcal{H}}^{2}}\right)\leq\frac{4}{\lambda}\left(\sqrt{\frac{K}{N}}+\sqrt{\frac{K}{M}}\right),

(68)

where the first inequality follows from Cauchy-Schwartz, and the last inequality from
Lemma

B.8
since
\|k(x,\cdot)\|_{\mathcal{H}}\leq\sqrt{K}
.
Therefore, combining (
66
), (
67
) and (
68
), we have

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} (ii)\leq 2\sqrt{K_{1d}}\left(\frac{\sqrt{K_{1d}}}{\lambda}c_{n}+\frac{2K^{3/2}}{\sqrt{M}\lambda^{2}}+\frac{2\sqrt{K}}{\sqrt{N}\lambda}+\frac{2\sqrt{K}}{\sqrt{M}\lambda}\right).\end{aligned}

Combining
(i)
and
(ii)
, we have

\displaystyle c_{n+1}\leq c_{n}\left(1+\gamma(1+\lambda)\frac{2\sqrt{KK_{2d}}+2K_{1d}}{\lambda}\right)+2\gamma(1+\lambda)\sqrt{K_{1d}}\left(\frac{2K^{3/2}}{\sqrt{M}\lambda^{2}}+\frac{2\sqrt{K}}{\sqrt{M}\lambda}+\frac{2\sqrt{K}}{\sqrt{N}\lambda}\right).

Denoting
A=\frac{2\sqrt{KK_{1d}}}{\sqrt{KK_{2d}}+K_{1d}}
and
R=K_{1d}+\sqrt{KK_{2d}}
as constants that only depend on the kernel, and using the discrete Gronwall lemma (Lemma 26 from
Arbel et al. 2019
) along with
c_{0}=0
, we obtain

\displaystyle c_{n_{\max}}\leq A\left(\frac{K}{\sqrt{M}\lambda}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{M}}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\right)\left(\exp\left(\gamma n_{\max}\frac{2(1+\lambda)R}{\lambda}\right)-1\right).

Since
\E W_{2}(\hat{\mu}_{n},\bar{\mu}_{n})\leq\sqrt{\E W_{2}^{2}(\hat{\mu}_{n},\bar{\mu}_{n})}\leq c_{n}
, we reach

\displaystyle\E W_{2}(\hat{\mu}_{n_{max}},\bar{\mu}_{n_{max}})\leq A\left(\frac{K}{\sqrt{M}\lambda}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{M}}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\right)\left(\exp\left(\gamma n_{\max}\frac{2(1+\lambda)R}{\lambda}\right)-1\right).

Finally, the proof is completed by noting that the r.h.s. is monotonically decreasing in
\lambda
and therefore the r.h.s. can be bounded by replacing
\lambda
with
\tilde{\lambda}=\min\limits_{i=1,\ldots,n_{\max}}\lambda_{i}
.

10.14
Proof of
Proposition

6.1

By defining the following operators,

\displaystyle S_{x}:\mathcal{H}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}^{M},\quad f\rightarrow\frac{1}{\sqrt{M}}\left[f(x^{(1)}),\ldots,f(x^{(M)})\right]^{\top},

\displaystyle S_{x}^{\ast}:\mathbb{R}^{M}\rightarrow\mathcal{H},\quad\alpha\rightarrow\frac{1}{\sqrt{M}}\sum_{i=1}^{M}\alpha_{i}k(x^{(i)},\cdot),

\displaystyle S_{y}:\mathcal{H}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}^{N},\quad f\rightarrow\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\left[f(y^{(1)}),\ldots,f(y^{(N)})\right]^{\top},

\displaystyle S_{y}^{\ast}:\mathbb{R}^{N}\rightarrow\mathcal{H},\quad\alpha\rightarrow\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\alpha_{i}k(y^{(i)},\cdot).

Then we have

\displaystyle\Sigma_{\hat{\pi}}=S_{x}^{\ast}S_{x},\quad K_{xx}=MS_{x}S_{x}^{\ast},\quad K_{xy}=\sqrt{MN}S_{x}S_{y}^{\ast}.

Using these, note that

\displaystyle h_{\hat{\mu},\hat{\pi}}^{\ast}

\displaystyle=2\left(\Sigma_{\hat{\pi}}+\lambda\mathrm{I}\right)^{-1}\left(m_{\hat{\mu}}-m_{\hat{\pi}}\right)

\displaystyle=2\left(\frac{1}{M}\sum_{i=1}^{M}k\left(x^{(i)},\cdot\right)\otimes k\left(x^{(i)},\cdot\right)+\lambda\mathrm{I}\right)^{-1}\left(\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}k\left(y^{(i)},\cdot\right)-\frac{1}{M}\sum_{i=1}^{M}k\left(x^{(i)},\cdot\right)\right)

\displaystyle=2\Big(S_{x}^{\ast}S_{x}+\lambda\mathrm{I}\Big)^{-1}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}S_{y}^{\ast}\mathbbm{1}_{N}-\frac{1}{\sqrt{M}}S_{x}^{\ast}\mathbbm{1}_{M}\right).

(69)

From the Woodbury inversion lemma, we have that

\displaystyle\Big(S_{x}^{\ast}S_{x}+\lambda\mathrm{I}\Big)^{-1}=\frac{1}{\lambda}\mathrm{I}-\frac{1}{\lambda}S_{x}^{\ast}(S_{x}S_{x}^{\ast}+\lambda\mathrm{I})^{-1}S_{x}.

Plugging the above into (
69
), we obtain

\displaystyle h_{\hat{\mu},\hat{\pi}}^{\ast}

\displaystyle=2\left(\frac{1}{\lambda}\mathrm{I}-\frac{1}{\lambda}S_{x}^{\ast}(S_{x}S_{x}^{\ast}+\lambda\mathrm{I})^{-1}S_{x}\right)\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}S_{y}^{\ast}\mathbbm{1}_{N}-\frac{1}{\sqrt{M}}S_{x}^{\ast}\mathbbm{1}_{M}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{2}{\lambda}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}S_{y}^{\ast}\mathbbm{1}_{N}-\frac{1}{\sqrt{M}}S_{x}^{\ast}\mathbbm{1}_{M}\right)-\frac{2}{\lambda}S_{x}^{\ast}\left(\frac{1}{M}K_{xx}+\lambda\mathrm{I}\right)^{-1}\frac{1}{N\sqrt{M}}K_{xy}\mathbbm{1}_{N}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\frac{2}{\lambda}S_{x}^{\ast}\left(\frac{1}{M}K_{xx}+\lambda\mathrm{I}\right)^{-1}\frac{1}{M\sqrt{M}}K_{xx}\mathbbm{1}_{M}

\displaystyle=\frac{2}{N\lambda}k\left(\cdot,y^{1:N}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{N}-\frac{2}{M\lambda}k\left(\cdot,x^{1:M}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{M}-\frac{2}{N\lambda}k\left(\cdot,x^{1:M}\right)\left(K_{xx}+M\lambda\mathrm{I}\right)^{-1}K_{xy}\mathbbm{1}_{N}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\frac{2}{M\lambda}k\left(\cdot,x^{1:M}\right)\left(K_{xx}+M\lambda\mathrm{I}\right)^{-1}K_{xx}\mathbbm{1}_{M}.

(70)

Obtaining
\operatorname{DrMMD}(\hat{\mu}\|\hat{\pi})
is then easy with
h_{\hat{\mu},\hat{\pi}}^{\ast}
shown in (
70
).

\displaystyle\quad\operatorname{DrMMD}(\hat{\mu}\|\hat{\pi})=(1+\lambda)\left\|\left(\Sigma_{\hat{\pi}}+\lambda\mathrm{I}\right)^{-\frac{1}{2}}\left(m_{\hat{\mu}}-m_{\hat{\pi}}\right)\right\|_{\mathcal{H}}^{2}

\displaystyle=(1+\lambda)\left\langle\frac{1}{2}h_{\hat{\mu},\hat{\pi}}^{*},m_{\hat{\mu}}-m_{\hat{\pi}}\right\rangle_{\mathcal{H}}

\displaystyle=(1+\lambda)\left\langle\frac{1}{2}h_{\hat{\mu},\hat{\pi}}^{*},\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}k\left(y^{(i)},\cdot\right)-\frac{1}{M}\sum_{i=1}^{M}k\left(x^{(i)},\cdot\right)\right\rangle_{\mathcal{H}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1+\lambda}{\lambda}\Bigg(\frac{1}{N^{2}}\mathbbm{1}_{N}^{\top}K_{yy}\mathbbm{1}_{N}+\frac{1}{M^{2}}\mathbbm{1}_{M}^{\top}K_{xx}\mathbbm{1}_{M}-\frac{2}{MN}\mathbbm{1}_{M}^{\top}K_{xy}\mathbbm{1}_{N}

\displaystyle\qquad-\frac{1}{N^{2}}\mathbbm{1}_{N}^{\top}K_{xy}^{\top}\left(K_{xx}+M\lambda\mathrm{I}\right)^{-1}K_{xy}\mathbbm{1}_{N}+\frac{2}{NM}\mathbbm{1}_{M}^{\top}K_{xx}\left(K_{xx}+M\lambda\mathrm{I}\right)^{-1}K_{xy}\mathbbm{1}_{N}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad-\frac{1}{M^{2}}\mathbbm{1}_{M}^{\top}K_{xx}\left(K_{xx}+M\lambda\mathrm{I}\right)^{-1}K_{xx}\mathbbm{1}_{M}\Bigg).

(71)

\operatorname{DrMMD}
and related functionals. Zonghao Chen is supported by the Engineering and Physical Sciences Research Council (EPSRC) through grant [EP/S021566/1]. Aratrika Mustafi and Bharath K.
